[18] (Normal) ׳ ׳™׳”׳•׳ ׳×׳ ׳•׳¢׳” ׳¢׳™׳¨׳•׳ ׳™׳× ׳‘׳¦׳׳×׳™׳ ׳׳”׳•׳•׳” ׳‘׳¢׳™׳” ׳׳•׳¨׳›׳‘׳× ׳”׳ ׳•׳‘׳¢׳× ׳׳”׳’׳™׳“׳•׳ ׳”׳׳×׳׳™׳“ ׳‘׳׳¡׳₪׳¨ ׳›׳׳™ ׳”׳¨׳›׳‘, ׳׳”׳©׳•׳ ׳•׳× ׳”׳’׳‘׳•׳”׳” ׳‘׳¢׳•׳׳¡׳™ ׳”׳×׳ ׳•׳¢׳” ׳׳׳•׳¨׳ ׳©׳¢׳•׳× ׳”׳™׳׳׳” ׳•׳׳”׳¦׳•׳¨׳ ׳‘׳׳×׳ ׳׳¢׳ ׳” ׳׳™׳™׳“׳™ ׳׳׳¦׳‘׳™ ׳—׳™׳¨׳•׳.׳׳¢׳¨׳›׳× ׳¨׳׳–׳•׳¨׳™׳ ׳™׳¢׳™׳׳” ׳ ׳“׳¨׳©׳× ׳׳׳–׳ ׳‘׳™׳ ׳׳™׳׳•׳¦׳™׳ ׳׳×׳—׳¨׳™׳ ׳‘׳–׳׳ ׳׳׳×, ׳×׳•׳ ׳©׳׳™׳¨׳” ׳¢׳ ׳‘׳˜׳™׳—׳•׳× ׳”׳׳©׳×׳׳©׳™׳ ׳•׳׳™׳§׳¡׳•׳ ׳§׳™׳‘׳•׳׳× ׳”׳¦׳•׳׳×.׳₪׳¨׳§ ׳–׳” ׳¡׳•׳§׳¨ ׳׳× ׳”׳’׳™׳©׳•׳× ׳”׳§׳™׳™׳׳•׳× ׳׳₪׳×׳¨׳•׳ ׳”׳‘׳¢׳™׳”, ׳”׳—׳ ׳׳©׳™׳˜׳•׳× ׳׳¡׳•׳¨׳×׳™׳•׳× ׳•׳¢׳“ ׳׳׳¢׳¨׳›׳•׳× ׳׳“׳₪׳˜׳™׳‘׳™׳•׳× ׳׳×׳§׳“׳׳•׳× ׳•׳׳ ׳×׳— ׳׳× ׳”׳‘׳¢׳™׳” ׳׳ ׳§׳•׳“׳× ׳׳‘׳˜ ׳©׳ ׳׳“׳¢׳™ ׳”׳׳—׳©׳‘ ׳›׳׳¢׳¨׳›׳× ׳׳ ׳™׳”׳•׳ ׳׳©׳׳‘׳™׳ ׳׳©׳•׳×׳₪׳™׳.
[19] (Normal) ׳’׳™׳©׳•׳× ׳׳¡׳•׳¨׳×׳™׳•׳× ׳׳ ׳™׳”׳•׳ ׳¨׳׳–׳•׳¨׳™׳׳׳׳•׳¨׳ ׳”׳©׳ ׳™׳ ׳₪׳•׳×׳—׳• ׳•׳™׳•׳©׳׳• ׳₪׳×׳¨׳•׳ ׳•׳× ׳©׳•׳ ׳™׳ ׳׳•׳•׳™׳¡׳•׳× ׳”׳×׳ ׳•׳¢׳” ׳‘׳¦׳׳×׳™׳:

[23] (a7) ׳¨׳׳–׳•׳¨׳™׳ ׳׳‘׳•׳¡׳¡׳™׳ ׳׳•׳׳׳•׳× ׳”׳©׳¨׳׳”:׳©׳™׳˜׳” ׳–׳• ׳׳‘׳•׳¡׳¡׳× ׳¢׳ ׳׳•׳׳׳•׳× ׳”׳©׳¨׳׳” ׳”׳׳•׳˜׳׳¢׳•׳× ׳‘׳×׳•׳ ׳”׳›׳‘׳™׳©.׳׳•׳׳׳•׳× ׳׳׳• ׳׳–׳”׳•׳× ׳©׳™׳ ׳•׳™ ׳‘׳©׳“׳” ׳”׳׳’׳ ׳˜׳™ ׳›׳׳©׳¨ ׳›׳׳™ ׳¨׳›׳‘ ׳¢׳•׳׳“ ׳׳¢׳׳™׳”׳, ׳•׳‘׳›׳ ׳”׳™׳ ׳׳׳₪׳©׳¨׳× ׳׳¨׳׳–׳•׳¨ ׳׳–׳”׳•׳× ׳ ׳•׳›׳—׳•׳× ׳›׳׳™ ׳¨׳›׳‘ ׳•׳׳£ ׳׳”׳¢׳¨׳™׳ ׳¢׳•׳׳¡ ׳™׳—׳¡׳™ ׳‘׳ ׳×׳™׳‘.׳™׳×׳¨׳•׳ ׳•׳×:1. ׳˜׳›׳ ׳•׳׳•׳’׳™׳” ׳׳•׳›׳—׳× ׳•׳™׳¦׳™׳‘׳”.2. ׳×׳’׳•׳‘׳” ׳׳׳™׳ ׳” ׳׳ ׳•׳›׳—׳•׳× ׳›׳׳™ ׳¨׳›׳‘ ׳’׳ ׳‘׳×׳ ׳׳™ ׳×׳ ׳•׳¢׳” ׳•׳׳–׳’ ׳׳•׳•׳™׳¨ ׳§׳©׳™׳.׳—׳¡׳¨׳•׳ ׳•׳×:1. ׳”׳×׳§׳ ׳” ׳•׳×׳—׳–׳•׳§׳” ׳™׳§׳¨׳•׳× ׳”׳“׳•׳¨׳©׳•׳× ׳—׳₪׳™׳¨׳” ׳‘׳›׳‘׳™׳©.2. ׳—׳•׳¡׳¨ ׳’׳׳™׳©׳•׳× ג€“ ׳§׳©׳” ׳׳©׳ ׳•׳× ׳₪׳¨׳™׳¡׳” ׳׳• ׳׳”׳¨׳—׳™׳‘ ׳׳× ׳”׳׳¢׳¨׳›׳×.3. ׳׳ ׳׳¡׳₪׳§׳× ׳׳™׳“׳¢ ׳›׳׳•׳×׳™ ׳׳“׳•׳™׳™׳§.4. ׳™׳™׳×׳›׳ ׳•׳›׳׳™ ׳¨׳›׳‘ ׳›׳’׳•׳ ׳׳•׳₪׳ ׳•׳¢׳™׳ ׳׳ ׳™׳–׳•׳”׳• ׳›׳™׳•׳•׳ ׳©׳”׳©׳™׳ ׳•׳™ ׳”׳׳’׳ ׳˜׳™ ׳©׳”׳ ׳™׳•׳¦׳¨׳™׳ ׳§׳˜׳ ׳׳™׳“׳™ ׳׳•    ׳©׳׳™׳ ׳ ׳¢׳•׳׳“׳™׳ ׳‘׳׳¨׳›׳– ׳”׳׳•׳׳׳”.
[24] (Normal) ׳’׳™׳©׳•׳× ׳׳×׳§׳“׳׳•׳× ׳׳ ׳™׳”׳•׳ ׳¨׳׳–׳•׳¨׳™׳
[25] (a7) ׳׳¢׳¨׳›׳•׳× ׳¨׳׳–׳•׳¨׳™׳ ׳׳“׳₪׳˜׳™׳‘׳™׳•׳× ׳׳–׳•׳¨׳™׳•׳× (SCOOT/SCATS):׳׳¢׳¨׳›׳•׳× ׳׳׳• ׳₪׳•׳¢׳׳•׳× ׳‘׳׳¡׳₪׳¨ ׳¢׳¨׳™׳ ׳’׳“׳•׳׳•׳× ׳‘׳¢׳•׳׳ ׳•׳׳×׳‘׳¡׳¡׳•׳× ׳¢׳ ׳׳™׳¡׳•׳£ ׳ ׳×׳•׳ ׳™׳ ׳׳—׳™׳™׳©׳ ׳™׳ ׳©׳•׳ ׳™׳ ׳‘׳¦׳׳×׳™׳. ׳”׳׳¢׳¨׳›׳•׳× ׳׳ ׳×׳—׳•׳× ׳׳× ׳”׳¢׳•׳׳¡׳™׳ ׳•׳׳×׳׳™׳׳•׳× ׳׳× ׳–׳׳ ׳™ ׳”׳¨׳׳–׳•׳¨׳™׳ ׳׳ ׳¨׳§ ׳‘׳¦׳•׳׳× ׳‘׳•׳“׳“׳× ׳׳׳ ׳‘׳¨׳׳” ׳׳–׳•׳¨׳™׳×, ׳×׳•׳ ׳ ׳™׳¡׳™׳•׳ ׳׳©׳׳•׳¨ ׳¢׳ ׳–׳¨׳™׳׳” ׳¦׳₪׳•׳₪׳”. ׳”׳׳¢׳¨׳›׳× ׳׳×׳‘׳¡׳¡׳× ׳¢׳ ׳׳•׳׳׳× ׳”׳©׳¨׳׳”, ׳׳¦׳׳׳•׳×, ׳—׳™׳™׳©׳ ׳™ ׳¨׳“׳׳¨ ׳•׳¢׳•׳“ ׳—׳™׳™׳©׳ ׳™׳ ׳ ׳•׳¡׳₪׳™׳.׳™׳×׳¨׳•׳ ׳•׳×:1. ׳©׳™׳₪׳•׳¨ ׳׳©׳׳¢׳•׳×׳™ ׳©׳ ׳–׳¨׳™׳׳× ׳”׳×׳ ׳•׳¢׳” ׳’׳ ׳‘׳–׳׳ ׳™ ׳¢׳•׳׳¡.2. ׳”׳×׳׳׳” ׳“׳™׳ ׳׳׳™׳× ׳׳©׳™׳ ׳•׳™׳™ ׳”׳×׳ ׳•׳¢׳” ׳‘׳׳”׳׳ ׳”׳™׳•׳.׳—׳¡׳¨׳•׳ ׳•׳×:1. ׳׳¢׳¨׳›׳•׳× ׳™׳§׳¨׳•׳× ׳•׳׳•׳¨׳›׳‘׳•׳× ׳׳”׳₪׳¢׳׳”.2. ׳×׳׳•׳™׳•׳× ׳‘׳×׳©׳×׳™׳× ׳×׳§׳©׳•׳¨׳× ׳¨׳¦׳™׳₪׳” ׳•׳‘׳×׳—׳–׳•׳§׳” ׳׳§׳¦׳•׳¢׳™׳× ׳׳×׳׳“׳×.
[27] (a7) ׳׳¢׳¨׳›׳× RHODES (Real- Time Hierarchical Optimized Distributed Effective System)׳׳¢׳¨׳›׳× ׳”׳₪׳•׳¢׳׳× ׳‘׳׳¨׳›׳™׳˜׳§׳˜׳•׳¨׳” ׳”׳™׳¨׳¨׳›׳™׳× ׳•׳׳‘׳¦׳¢׳× ׳׳•׳₪׳˜׳™׳׳™׳–׳¦׳™׳” ׳‘׳–׳׳ ׳׳׳× ׳¢׳ ׳‘׳¡׳™׳¡ ׳—׳™׳–׳•׳™ ׳¢׳•׳׳¡׳™׳ ׳¢׳×׳™׳“׳™׳™׳. ׳”׳׳¢׳¨׳›׳× ׳׳ ׳¡׳” ׳׳—׳–׳•׳× ׳׳× ׳–׳¨׳™׳׳× ׳”׳×׳ ׳•׳¢׳” ׳׳˜׳•׳•׳— ׳”׳׳¨׳•׳ ׳™׳•׳×׳¨ ׳•׳׳§׳‘׳ ׳”׳—׳׳˜׳•׳× ׳”׳׳‘׳•׳¡׳¡׳•׳× ׳¢׳ ׳¦׳₪׳™ ׳”׳’׳¢׳× ׳¨׳›׳‘׳™׳ ׳•׳׳ ׳¨׳§ ׳¢׳ ׳”׳׳¦׳‘ ׳”׳ ׳•׳›׳—׳™.׳™׳×׳¨׳•׳ ׳•׳×:1. ׳—׳™׳–׳•׳™ ׳¦׳ ׳•׳¢׳” ׳¢׳×׳™׳“׳™׳×.2. ׳׳§׳‘׳ ׳”׳—׳׳˜׳•׳× ׳‘׳–׳׳ ׳׳׳×.3. ׳×׳™׳׳•׳ ׳‘׳™׳ ׳¦׳׳×׳™׳.4. ׳”׳₪׳—׳×׳× ׳¢׳•׳׳¡׳™׳.׳—׳¡׳¨׳•׳ ׳•׳×:1. ׳׳¢׳¨׳›׳× ׳׳•׳¨׳›׳‘׳× ׳׳׳•׳“.2. ׳‘׳¢׳ ׳—׳™׳©׳•׳‘׳™׳ ׳›׳‘׳“׳™׳.3. ׳¢׳׳•׳× ׳’׳‘׳•׳”׳”.4. ׳×׳׳•׳× ׳‘׳ ׳×׳•׳ ׳™ ׳—׳™׳™׳©׳ ׳™׳.
[29] (Normal) ׳”׳׳¢׳¨׳›׳× ׳”׳׳•׳¦׳¢׳× ׳ ׳“׳¨׳©׳× ׳׳”׳×׳׳•׳“׳“ ׳¢׳ ׳׳¨׳‘׳¢׳” ׳׳×׳’׳¨׳™׳ ׳׳¨׳›׳–׳™׳™׳ ׳‘׳ ׳™׳”׳•׳ ׳׳©׳׳‘׳™׳:
[30] (a7) ׳׳ ׳™׳¢׳” ׳”׳“׳“׳™׳× (Mutual Exclusion):׳–׳”׳• ׳×׳ ׳׳™ ׳”׳‘׳˜׳™׳—׳•׳× ׳”׳‘׳¡׳™׳¡׳™ ׳‘׳™׳•׳×׳¨. ׳”׳׳¢׳¨׳›׳× ׳—׳™׳™׳‘׳× ׳׳”׳‘׳˜׳™׳— ׳׳×׳׳˜׳™׳× ׳©׳׳ ׳™׳™׳•׳•׳¦׳¨ ׳׳¦׳‘ ׳‘׳• ׳©׳ ׳™ ׳–׳¨׳׳™׳ ׳׳×׳ ׳’׳©׳™׳ ׳׳§׳‘׳׳™׳ ׳’׳™׳©׳” ׳׳׳©׳׳‘ (׳׳•׳¨ ׳™׳¨׳•׳§) ׳‘׳• ׳–׳׳ ׳™׳×. ׳›׳™׳©׳׳•׳ ׳‘׳׳›׳™׳₪׳× ׳׳ ׳™׳¢׳” ׳”׳“׳“׳™׳× ׳׳•׳‘׳™׳ ׳‘׳”׳›׳¨׳— ׳׳×׳׳•׳ ׳”.
[31] (a7) ׳׳ ׳™׳¢׳× ׳”׳¨׳¢׳‘׳” (Starvation Prevention) ׳•׳”׳•׳’׳ ׳•׳× (Fairness):׳‘׳׳¢׳¨׳›׳•׳× ׳¢׳׳•׳¡׳•׳× ׳§׳™׳™׳ ׳¡׳™׳›׳•׳ ׳©׳ ׳×׳™׳‘ ׳¨׳׳©׳™ ׳•׳¢׳׳•׳¡ ׳™׳§׳‘׳ ׳¢׳“׳™׳₪׳•׳× ׳׳×׳׳“׳×, ׳‘׳¢׳•׳“ ׳ ׳×׳™׳‘ ׳¦׳“׳“׳™ "׳™׳¨׳¢׳‘"׳•׳׳ ׳™׳§׳‘׳ ׳–׳׳ ׳™׳¨׳•׳§. ׳”׳׳¢׳¨׳›׳× ׳ ׳“׳¨׳©׳× ׳׳™׳™׳©׳ ׳׳׳’׳•׳¨׳™׳×׳ ׳×׳–׳׳•׳ (Scheduling Algorithm) ׳”׳׳‘׳˜׳™׳— ׳”׳•׳’׳ ׳•׳×, ׳›׳ ׳©׳›׳ ׳‘׳§׳©׳” ׳׳׳©׳׳‘ ׳×׳¢׳ ׳” ׳×׳•׳ ׳–׳׳ ׳¡׳‘׳™׳¨ (Bounded Waiting), ׳’׳ ׳׳ ׳”׳“׳‘׳¨ ׳₪׳•׳’׳¢ ׳׳¢׳˜ ׳‘׳™׳¢׳™׳׳•׳× ׳”׳›׳•׳׳׳×.
[32] (a7) ׳׳ ׳™׳¢׳× ׳§׳™׳₪׳׳•׳ (Deadlock):׳׳¦׳‘ ׳©׳ ׳§׳™׳₪׳׳•׳ ׳¢׳׳•׳ ׳׳”׳™׳•׳•׳¦׳¨ ׳›׳׳©׳¨ ׳¦׳׳×׳™׳ ׳¡׳׳•׳›׳™׳ ׳—׳•׳¡׳׳™׳ ׳–׳” ׳׳× ׳–׳” (׳׳׳©׳, ׳¦׳•׳׳× ׳' ׳׳׳ ׳‘׳¨׳›׳‘׳™׳ ׳”׳׳ ׳¡׳™׳ ׳׳”׳’׳™׳¢ ׳׳¦׳•׳׳× ׳‘' ׳•׳¦׳•׳׳× ׳‘' ׳׳׳ ׳‘׳¨׳›׳‘׳™׳ ׳”׳׳ ׳¡׳™׳ ׳׳”׳’׳™׳¢ ׳׳¦׳•׳׳× ׳'). ׳”׳׳¢׳¨׳›׳× ׳ ׳“׳¨׳©׳× ׳׳›׳׳•׳ ׳׳ ׳’׳ ׳•׳ ׳™ ׳×׳§׳©׳•׳¨׳× ׳•׳¡׳ ׳›׳¨׳•׳ ׳‘׳—׳ ׳¦׳׳×׳™׳ ׳›׳“׳™ ׳׳–׳”׳•׳× ׳•׳׳׳ ׳•׳¢ ׳׳¦׳‘׳™׳ ׳׳׳• (׳₪׳§׳§׳™ ׳©׳¨׳©׳¨׳×) ׳•׳׳•׳•׳“׳ ׳©׳×׳׳™׳“ ׳§׳™׳™׳׳× ׳׳₪׳©׳¨׳•׳× ׳׳”׳×׳§׳“׳׳•׳× (Liveness).
[33] (a7) ׳”׳₪׳§׳¢׳× ׳׳©׳׳‘ (Preemption) ׳׳˜׳•׳‘׳× ׳¨׳›׳‘׳™ ׳—׳™׳¨׳•׳:׳‘׳“׳•׳׳” ׳׳׳¢׳¨׳›׳× ׳”׳₪׳¢׳׳” ׳”׳׳˜׳₪׳׳× ׳‘׳₪׳¡׳™׳§׳•׳× (Interrupts), ׳”׳׳¢׳¨׳›׳× ׳—׳™׳™׳‘׳× ׳׳×׳׳•׳ ׳‘׳׳ ׳’׳ ׳•׳ Preemption. ׳›׳׳©׳¨ ׳׳×׳§׳‘׳׳× ׳“׳¨׳™׳©׳” ׳׳¨׳›׳‘ ׳—׳™׳¨׳•׳ (׳×׳”׳׳™׳ ׳‘׳¢׳“׳™׳₪׳•׳× ׳¢׳׳™׳•׳ ׳”), ׳”׳׳¢׳¨׳›׳× ׳¦׳¨׳™׳›׳” ׳׳”׳™׳•׳× ׳׳¡׳•׳’׳׳× ׳׳”׳₪׳¡׳™׳§ ׳‘׳‘׳˜׳—׳” ׳׳× ׳”׳×׳”׳׳™׳ ׳”׳¨׳¥ (׳”׳×׳ ׳•׳¢׳” ׳”׳¨׳’׳™׳׳”), ׳׳₪׳ ׳•׳× ׳׳× ׳”׳׳©׳׳‘ ׳‘׳׳•׳₪׳ ׳׳™׳™׳“׳™ ׳•׳׳”׳¢׳ ׳™׳§ ׳׳•׳×׳• ׳׳¨׳›׳‘ ׳”׳—׳™׳¨׳•׳, ׳×׳•׳ ׳©׳׳™׳¨׳” ׳¢׳ ׳–׳׳ ׳™ ׳׳¢׳‘׳¨ ׳‘׳˜׳•׳—׳™׳ (Inter-green times).
[35] (Normal) ׳¡׳™׳›׳•׳׳׳”׳¡׳§׳™׳¨׳” ׳”׳ "׳ ׳¢׳•׳׳” ׳›׳™ ׳§׳™׳™׳׳™׳ ׳₪׳×׳¨׳•׳ ׳•׳× ׳׳©׳¨ ׳׳¦׳™׳¢׳™׳ ׳׳¢׳ ׳” ׳—׳׳§׳™ ׳׳‘׳¢׳™׳” ׳”׳׳•׳¦׳’׳×, ׳—׳׳§׳ ׳™׳¢׳™׳׳™׳ ׳׳ ׳™׳§׳¨׳™׳ ׳•׳׳•׳¨׳›׳‘׳™׳ ׳•׳׳—׳¨׳™׳ ׳₪׳©׳•׳˜׳™׳ ׳׳ ׳—׳¡׳¨׳™ ׳’׳׳™׳©׳•׳×.׳”׳₪׳¢׳¨ ׳”׳׳¨׳›׳–׳™ ׳”׳¢׳•׳׳” ׳׳”׳’׳™׳©׳•׳× ׳”׳§׳™׳™׳׳•׳× ׳”׳•׳ ׳”׳¦׳•׳¨׳ ׳‘׳©׳™׳׳•׳‘ ׳‘׳™׳ ׳ ׳™׳”׳•׳ ׳×׳ ׳•׳¢׳” ׳׳“׳₪׳˜׳™׳‘׳™, ׳×׳™׳׳•׳ ׳‘׳™׳ ׳¦׳׳×׳™׳ ׳•׳׳×׳ ׳§׳“׳™׳׳•׳× ׳׳׳¦׳‘׳™ ׳—׳™׳¨׳•׳ ׳×׳•׳ ׳©׳׳™׳¨׳” ׳¢׳ ׳₪׳©׳˜׳•׳× ׳™׳—׳¡׳™׳× ׳•׳™׳›׳•׳׳× ׳¢׳‘׳•׳“׳” ׳‘׳–׳׳ ׳׳׳×.׳₪׳¨׳•׳™׳§׳˜ ׳–׳” ׳ ׳•׳¢׳“ ׳׳”׳×׳׳•׳“׳“ ׳¢׳ ׳₪׳¢׳¨ ׳–׳” ׳‘׳׳׳¦׳¢׳•׳× ׳׳¢׳¨׳›׳× ׳׳‘׳•׳¡׳¡׳× ׳¨׳׳™׳™׳” ׳׳׳•׳—׳©׳‘׳× ׳•׳×׳§׳©׳•׳¨׳× ׳—׳›׳׳” ׳”׳׳™׳™׳©׳׳× ׳¢׳§׳¨׳•׳ ׳•׳× ׳©׳ ׳ ׳™׳”׳•׳ ׳׳©׳׳‘׳™׳ ׳“׳™׳ ׳׳׳™ ׳›׳“׳™ ׳׳¡׳₪׳§ ׳₪׳×׳¨׳•׳ ׳’׳׳™׳©, ׳–׳•׳ ׳׳™׳™׳©׳•׳ ׳•׳‘׳˜׳•׳— ׳¢׳ ׳“׳’׳© ׳׳™׳•׳—׳“ ׳¢׳ ׳×׳™׳¢׳“׳•׳£ ׳¨׳›׳‘׳™ ׳—׳™׳¨׳•׳ ׳•׳׳ ׳™׳¢׳× ׳§׳™׳₪׳׳•׳ ׳¨׳©׳×׳™.

[131] (Normal) ׳׳¢׳¨׳›׳•׳× ׳ ׳™׳”׳•׳ ׳”׳×׳ ׳•׳¢׳” ׳”׳׳¡׳•׳¨׳×׳™׳•׳× ׳‘׳¢׳¨׳™׳ ׳¨׳‘׳•׳× ׳׳‘׳•׳¡׳¡׳•׳× ׳¢׳ ׳׳ ׳’׳ ׳•׳ ׳™׳ ׳¡׳˜׳˜׳™׳™׳ ׳•׳§׳©׳™׳—׳™׳ (׳›׳’׳•׳ ׳˜׳™׳™׳׳¨׳™׳ ׳§׳‘׳•׳¢׳™׳ ׳׳¨׳׳© ׳׳• ׳—׳™׳™׳©׳ ׳™׳ ׳‘׳™׳ ׳׳¨׳™׳™׳ ׳׳§׳•׳׳™׳™׳), ׳©׳׳™׳ ׳ ׳׳¡׳•׳’׳׳™׳ ׳׳©׳§׳£ ׳׳× ׳¢׳•׳׳¡׳™ ׳”׳×׳ ׳•׳¢׳” ׳”׳׳׳™׳×׳™׳™׳ ׳•׳”׳׳©׳×׳ ׳™׳ ׳‘׳₪׳•׳¢׳ ׳׳׳•׳¨׳ ׳©׳¢׳•׳× ׳”׳™׳׳׳”. ׳׳¦׳‘ ׳–׳” ׳׳™׳™׳¦׳¨ ׳—׳•׳¡׳¨ ׳™׳¢׳™׳׳•׳× ׳׳•׳‘׳ ׳”, ׳”׳׳•׳‘׳™׳ ׳׳”׳׳×׳ ׳•׳× ׳©׳•׳•׳ ׳׳׳•׳©׳›׳•׳× ׳©׳ ׳ ׳”׳’׳™׳ ׳•׳”׳•׳׳›׳™ ׳¨׳’׳ ׳‘׳ ׳×׳™׳‘׳™׳ ׳₪׳ ׳•׳™׳™׳, ׳׳”׳’׳‘׳¨׳× ׳’׳•׳“׳© ׳”׳×׳ ׳•׳¢׳” (׳₪׳§׳§׳™ ׳©׳¨׳©׳¨׳×), ׳׳¢׳׳™׳™׳” ׳‘׳¦׳¨׳™׳›׳× ׳”׳“׳׳§ ׳•׳‘׳–׳™׳”׳•׳ ׳”׳׳•׳•׳™׳¨, ׳•׳‘׳׳§׳¨׳™׳ ׳”׳§׳™׳¦׳•׳ ׳™׳™׳ ׳‘׳™׳•׳×׳¨ ג€“ ׳׳¢׳™׳›׳•׳‘ ׳§׳¨׳™׳˜׳™ ׳©׳ ׳¨׳›׳‘׳™ ׳”׳¦׳׳” ׳•׳‘׳™׳˜׳—׳•׳.

[153] (a7) ׳׳™׳¡׳•׳£ ׳•׳¢׳™׳‘׳•׳“ ׳ ׳×׳•׳ ׳™׳ ׳‘׳–׳׳ ׳׳׳×: ׳§׳׳™׳˜׳” ׳¨׳¦׳™׳₪׳” ׳©׳ ׳ ׳×׳•׳ ׳™ ׳”׳ ׳×׳™׳‘׳™׳ (׳›׳’׳•׳ ׳›׳׳•׳× ׳›׳׳™ ׳¨׳›׳‘, ׳–׳׳ ׳™ ׳”׳׳×׳ ׳” ׳•׳›׳™׳•׳•׳ ׳™ ׳ ׳¡׳™׳¢׳”) ׳•׳¢׳“׳›׳•׳ ׳©׳•׳˜׳£ ׳©׳ ׳×׳׳•׳ ׳× ׳”׳׳¦׳‘ ׳‘׳¦׳•׳׳×.
[155] (a7) ׳׳ ׳’׳ ׳•׳ ׳”׳—׳׳˜׳” ׳“׳™׳ ׳׳™ ׳׳¨׳׳–׳•׳¨: ׳׳™׳׳•׳© ׳׳׳’׳•׳¨׳™׳×׳ ׳×׳™׳¢׳“׳•׳£ ׳׳×׳§׳“׳ ׳”׳׳©׳ ׳” ׳׳× ׳”׳₪׳׳–׳•׳× ׳‘׳”׳×׳׳ ׳׳¢׳•׳׳¡ ׳‘׳₪׳•׳¢׳ ׳•׳‘׳–׳׳ ׳”׳׳×׳ ׳” ׳׳¦׳˜׳‘׳¨, ׳›׳—׳׳•׳₪׳” ׳׳×׳–׳׳•׳ ׳§׳©׳™׳— ׳•׳§׳‘׳•׳¢ ׳׳¨׳׳©.
[157] (a7) ׳©׳׳™׳¨׳” ׳¢׳ ׳׳™׳–׳•׳ ׳•׳”׳•׳’׳ ׳•׳× ׳‘׳™׳ ׳ ׳×׳™׳‘׳™׳: ׳׳ ׳™׳¢׳× ׳׳¦׳‘׳™ ׳§׳™׳¦׳•׳ ׳©׳‘׳”׳ ׳ ׳×׳™׳‘ ׳‘׳•׳“׳“ ׳׳• ׳¦׳™׳¨ ׳׳¨׳›׳–׳™ ׳׳§׳‘׳׳™׳ ׳¢׳“׳™׳₪׳•׳× ׳§׳‘׳•׳¢׳” ׳¢׳ ׳—׳©׳‘׳•׳ ׳”׳¦׳™׳¨׳™׳ ׳”׳׳©׳ ׳™׳™׳, ׳•׳–׳׳× ׳‘׳׳׳¦׳¢׳•׳× ׳”׳˜׳׳¢׳× ׳›׳׳׳™ ׳׳™׳–׳•׳ ׳§׳₪׳“׳ ׳™׳™׳ ׳‘׳׳—׳–׳•׳¨׳™ ׳”׳¨׳׳–׳•׳¨.
[158] (a7) ׳×׳׳™׳›׳” ׳‘׳×׳¨׳—׳™׳©׳™ ׳—׳™׳¨׳•׳ (Emergency Preemption): ׳–׳™׳”׳•׳™ ׳׳•׳˜׳•׳׳˜׳™ ׳©׳ ׳ ׳×׳™׳‘ ׳©׳‘׳• ׳׳×׳§׳¨׳‘ ׳¨׳›׳‘ ׳—׳™׳¨׳•׳ ׳•׳׳×׳ ׳§׳“׳™׳׳•׳× ׳׳™׳™׳“׳™׳× ׳•׳׳‘׳•׳§׳¨׳× ׳׳¦׳™׳¨ ׳”׳¨׳׳•׳•׳ ׳˜׳™, ׳×׳•׳ ׳—׳–׳¨׳” ׳׳•׳‘׳ ׳™׳× ׳•׳׳¡׳•׳“׳¨׳× ׳׳©׳’׳¨׳” ׳׳™׳“ ׳¢׳ ׳¡׳™׳•׳ ׳”׳׳™׳¨׳•׳¢.
[159] (a7) ׳׳׳©׳§ ׳×׳¦׳•׳’׳” ׳‘׳–׳׳ ׳׳׳× (Dashboard): ׳”׳¦׳’׳” ׳׳™׳ ׳˜׳•׳׳™׳˜׳™׳‘׳™׳× ׳•׳‘׳¨׳•׳¨׳” ׳©׳ ׳׳¦׳‘ ׳”׳₪׳׳–׳•׳× ׳”׳ ׳•׳›׳—׳™׳•׳×, ׳׳“׳“׳™ ׳”׳¢׳•׳׳¡, ׳•׳–׳׳ ׳™ ׳”׳׳×׳ ׳” ׳‘׳ ׳×׳™׳‘׳™׳ ׳”׳©׳•׳ ׳™׳ ׳׳˜׳•׳‘׳× ׳”׳׳©׳×׳׳©.
[160] (a7) ׳׳“׳™׳“׳” ׳•׳”׳¢׳¨׳›׳× ׳‘׳™׳¦׳•׳¢׳™׳: ׳‘׳“׳™׳§׳” ׳•׳›׳™׳׳•׳× ׳©׳ ׳©׳™׳₪׳•׳¨ ׳™׳¢׳™׳׳•׳× ׳”׳–׳¨׳™׳׳” ׳‘׳¦׳•׳׳× ׳‘׳׳׳¦׳¢׳•׳× ׳׳“׳“׳™׳ ׳׳•׳’׳“׳¨׳™׳, ׳›׳’׳•׳ ׳§׳™׳¦׳•׳¨ ׳–׳׳ ׳”׳”׳׳×׳ ׳” ׳”׳׳׳•׳¦׳¢, ׳”׳₪׳—׳×׳× ׳׳•׳¨׳ ׳”׳×׳•׳¨׳™׳ ׳•׳׳”׳™׳¨׳•׳× ׳”׳×׳’׳•׳‘׳” ׳׳©׳™׳ ׳•׳™׳™ ׳₪׳×׳¢ ׳‘׳¢׳•׳׳¡.
[161] (a7) ׳׳¨׳›׳™׳˜׳§׳˜׳•׳¨׳” ׳׳•׳“׳•׳׳¨׳™׳× ׳•׳ ׳™׳×׳ ׳× ׳׳”׳¨׳—׳‘׳”: ׳×׳›׳ ׳•׳ ׳•׳¨׳›׳™׳‘׳×׳• ׳©׳ ׳׳‘׳ ׳” ׳×׳•׳›׳ ׳” ׳׳•׳“׳•׳׳¨׳™, ׳”׳׳׳₪׳©׳¨ ׳‘׳¢׳×׳™׳“ ׳”׳•׳¡׳₪׳× ׳¦׳׳×׳™׳ ׳—׳“׳©׳™׳ ׳׳׳¢׳¨׳›׳×, ׳©׳™׳ ׳•׳™ ׳—׳•׳§׳™׳ ׳¢׳¡׳§׳™׳™׳ ׳•׳©׳“׳¨׳•׳’ ׳©׳ ׳׳׳’׳•׳¨׳™׳×׳׳™ ׳”׳ ׳™׳”׳•׳ ׳׳׳ ׳₪׳’׳™׳¢׳” ׳‘׳׳™׳‘׳× ׳”׳׳¢׳¨׳›׳×.

[164] (Normal) ׳׳•׳₪׳˜׳™׳׳™׳–׳¦׳™׳™׳× ׳׳׳’׳•׳¨׳™׳×׳ ׳”׳×׳–׳׳•׳: ׳׳™׳–׳•׳ ׳‘׳™׳ ׳™׳¢׳™׳׳•׳× ׳׳”׳•׳’׳ ׳•׳× ׳•׳™׳¦׳™׳‘׳•׳× ׳”׳׳—׳–׳•׳¨
[165] (Normal) ׳׳”׳•׳× ׳”׳׳×׳’׳¨ :׳”׳׳×׳’׳¨ ׳”׳׳¨׳›׳–׳™ ׳‘׳׳׳’׳•׳¨׳™׳×׳ ׳”׳‘׳§׳¨׳” ׳”׳™׳” ׳׳׳ ׳•׳¢ ׳׳¦׳‘ ׳©׳‘׳• ׳ ׳×׳™׳‘ ׳¢׳׳•׳¡ ׳‘׳׳™׳•׳—׳“ ׳׳§׳‘׳ ׳׳•׳¨ ׳™׳¨׳•׳§ ׳§׳‘׳•׳¢ ׳¢׳ ׳—׳©׳‘׳•׳ ׳ ׳×׳™׳‘׳™׳ ׳׳©׳ ׳™׳™׳, ׳׳” ׳©׳™׳•׳¦׳¨ "׳¨׳¢׳‘ ׳×׳¢׳‘׳•׳¨׳×׳™"  .(Starvation) ׳׳ ׳’׳“, ׳ ׳“׳¨׳© ׳׳׳ ׳•׳¢ ׳×׳•׳₪׳¢׳× ׳¨׳˜׳˜ ׳©׳ ׳₪׳׳–׳•׳× (Phase Oscillations) ג€“ ׳׳¦׳‘ ׳©׳‘׳• ׳”׳׳¢׳¨׳›׳× ׳׳—׳׳™׳₪׳” ׳׳¦׳‘׳™ ׳¨׳׳–׳•׳¨ ׳‘׳›׳ ׳›׳׳” ׳©׳ ׳™׳•׳× ׳¢׳§׳‘ ׳×׳ ׳•׳“׳•׳× ׳§׳˜׳ ׳•׳× ׳•׳–׳׳ ׳™׳•׳× ׳‘׳ ׳×׳•׳ ׳™ ׳”׳׳¦׳׳׳•׳×.
[166] (Normal) ׳”׳₪׳×׳¨׳•׳ ׳”׳™׳™׳©׳•׳׳™:׳₪׳•׳×׳— ׳׳׳’׳•׳¨׳™׳×׳ ׳©׳§׳׳•׳ ׳”׳׳©׳׳‘ ׳‘׳™׳ ׳ ׳₪׳— ׳”׳×׳ ׳•׳¢׳” ׳”׳ ׳•׳›׳—׳™ ׳׳‘׳™׳ ׳–׳׳ ׳”׳”׳׳×׳ ׳” ׳”׳׳¦׳˜׳‘׳¨ ׳©׳ ׳›׳׳™ ׳”׳¨׳›׳‘ ׳‘׳ ׳×׳™׳‘. ׳ ׳×׳™׳‘ ׳”׳׳׳×׳™׳ ׳–׳׳ ׳¨׳‘ ׳׳§׳‘׳ "׳‘׳•׳ ׳•׳¡ ׳¢׳“׳™׳₪׳•׳×" ׳׳×׳׳˜׳™ ׳”׳׳©׳§׳£ ׳×׳ ׳׳™ ׳”׳•׳’׳ ׳•׳×. ׳›׳“׳™ ׳׳”׳‘׳˜׳™׳— ׳׳× ׳™׳¦׳™׳‘׳•׳× ׳”׳׳—׳–׳•׳¨, ׳”׳•׳˜׳׳¢ ׳׳ ׳’׳ ׳•׳ ׳”׳©׳”׳™׳” ׳™׳™׳¢׳•׳“׳™ (Debounce/Hysteresis) ׳”׳׳’׳“׳™׳¨ ׳—׳¡׳ ׳•׳¡׳£ ׳׳™׳ ׳™׳׳׳™ ׳׳©׳™׳ ׳•׳™ ׳ ׳₪׳— ׳”׳׳¦׳“׳™׳§ ׳”׳—׳׳₪׳× ׳₪׳׳–׳”, ׳׳¦׳“ ׳§׳‘׳™׳¢׳× ׳׳’׳‘׳׳× ׳–׳׳ ׳׳™׳ ׳™׳׳׳™׳× ׳׳₪׳¢׳™׳׳•׳× ׳©׳ ׳›׳ ׳₪׳׳–׳”.(Safe Inter-green times)
[167] (Normal) ׳”׳’׳“׳¨׳× ׳₪׳•׳ ׳§׳¦׳™׳™׳× ׳”׳×׳’׳׳•׳ (Reward Function) ׳•׳׳•׳“׳ ׳”׳ ׳™׳§׳•׳“ ׳‘׳׳׳’׳•׳¨׳™׳×׳
[168] (Normal) ׳׳”׳•׳× ׳”׳׳×׳’׳¨: ׳›׳“׳™ ׳©׳¡׳•׳›׳ ׳׳׳™׳“׳× ׳”׳—׳™׳–׳•׳§ (RL) ׳™׳§׳‘׳ ׳”׳—׳׳˜׳•׳× ׳•׳™׳¡׳•׳× ׳ ׳›׳•׳ ׳•׳×, ׳ ׳“׳¨׳© ׳׳”׳’׳“׳™׳¨ ׳‘׳¦׳•׳¨׳” ׳‘׳¨׳•׳¨׳” ׳•׳”׳’׳™׳•׳ ׳™׳× ׳׳¢׳¨׳›׳× ׳ ׳§׳•׳“׳•׳× (׳×׳’׳׳•׳׳™׳ ׳•׳§׳ ׳¡׳•׳×) ׳׳›׳ ׳×׳¨׳—׳™׳© ׳‘׳¦׳•׳׳×. ׳”׳׳×׳’׳¨ ׳˜׳׳•׳ ׳‘׳›׳™׳•׳ ׳”׳׳©׳§׳׳™׳: ׳§׳‘׳™׳¢׳× ׳”"׳׳—׳™׳¨" ׳”׳׳×׳׳˜׳™ ׳©׳ ׳¨׳›׳‘ ׳׳׳×׳™׳ ׳׳¢׳•׳׳× ׳”׳•׳׳ ׳¨׳’׳, ׳•׳”׳’׳“׳¨׳× ׳§׳ ׳¡ ׳—׳¨׳™׳’ ׳¢׳ ׳¢׳™׳›׳•׳‘ ׳¨׳›׳‘ ׳—׳™׳¨׳•׳. ׳”׳’׳“׳¨׳” ׳׳ ׳׳׳•׳–׳ ׳× ׳©׳ ׳”׳ ׳§׳•׳“׳•׳× ׳¢׳׳•׳׳” ׳׳’׳¨׳•׳ ׳׳׳¢׳¨׳›׳× ׳׳₪׳¢׳•׳ ׳‘׳¦׳•׳¨׳” ׳§׳™׳¦׳•׳ ׳™׳× ׳׳• ׳׳ ׳”׳•׳’׳ ׳× ׳›׳׳₪׳™ ׳ ׳×׳™׳‘׳™׳ ׳׳¡׳•׳™׳׳™׳.
[169] (Normal) ׳”׳₪׳×׳¨׳•׳ ׳”׳™׳™׳©׳•׳׳™ :׳‘׳™׳¦׳•׳¢ ׳ ׳™׳×׳•׳— ׳׳×׳׳˜׳™ ׳•׳¡׳™׳׳•׳׳˜׳™׳‘׳™ ׳׳§׳“׳™׳ ׳׳§׳‘׳™׳¢׳× ׳¢׳¨׳›׳™ ׳”׳׳©׳§׳, ׳×׳•׳ ׳”׳˜׳׳¢׳× ׳₪׳•׳ ׳§׳¦׳™׳™׳× ׳§׳ ׳¡ ׳׳¢׳¨׳™׳›׳™׳× (Exponential Penalty) ׳”׳׳©׳§׳׳׳× ׳׳× ׳׳•׳¨׳ ׳”׳×׳•׳¨ ׳•׳–׳׳ ׳”׳”׳׳×׳ ׳” ׳”׳׳¦׳˜׳‘׳¨ ׳‘׳›׳ ׳ ׳×׳™׳‘ ׳‘׳ ׳₪׳¨׳“.
[170] (Normal) ׳ ׳™׳”׳•׳ ׳¡׳™׳‘׳•׳›׳™׳•׳× ׳”׳׳¢׳¨׳›׳× ׳•׳₪׳™׳¨׳•׳§ ׳׳•׳“׳•׳׳¨׳™ ׳©׳ ׳”׳₪׳¨׳•׳™׳§׳˜
[171] (Normal) ׳׳”׳•׳× ׳”׳׳×׳’׳¨:׳₪׳¨׳•׳™׳§׳˜ ׳”׳׳©׳׳‘ ׳׳¡׳₪׳¨ ׳¢׳•׳׳׳•׳× ׳×׳•׳›׳ ׳©׳•׳ ׳™׳ ג€“ ׳¨׳׳™׳™׳” ׳׳׳•׳—׳©׳‘׳×, ׳׳׳’׳•׳¨׳™׳×׳׳™׳§׳” ׳©׳ ׳׳׳™׳“׳× ׳—׳™׳–׳•׳§, ׳׳¢׳¨׳›׳•׳× ׳–׳׳ ׳׳׳× ׳•׳׳׳©׳§ Web ג€“ ׳׳׳•׳₪׳™׳™׳ ׳‘׳¡׳™׳‘׳•׳›׳™׳•׳× ׳׳¨׳›׳™׳˜׳§׳˜׳•׳ ׳™׳× ׳’׳‘׳•׳”׳” ׳׳׳•׳“. ׳”׳׳×׳’׳¨ ׳”׳׳¨׳›׳–׳™ ׳”׳™׳” ׳׳׳ ׳•׳¢ ׳”׳¦׳₪׳” ׳©׳ ׳׳™׳“׳¢, ׳׳”׳©׳×׳׳˜ ׳¢׳ ׳—׳׳§׳™ ׳”׳₪׳¨׳•׳™׳§׳˜ ׳”׳©׳•׳ ׳™׳ ׳•׳׳׳¦׳•׳ ׳׳× ׳”׳“׳¨׳ ׳׳—׳‘׳¨ ׳‘׳™׳ ׳”׳˜׳›׳ ׳•׳׳•׳’׳™׳•׳× ׳”׳©׳•׳ ׳•׳× ׳‘׳¦׳•׳¨׳” ׳—׳׳§׳” ׳•׳׳‘׳•׳§׳¨׳×.
[172] (Normal) ׳”׳₪׳×׳¨׳•׳ ׳”׳™׳™׳©׳•׳׳™:׳₪׳™׳¨׳•׳§ ׳”׳׳¢׳¨׳›׳× ׳”׳’׳“׳•׳׳” ׳׳×׳×׳™-׳׳¢׳¨׳›׳•׳× ׳¢׳¦׳׳׳™׳•׳× ׳•׳׳‘׳•׳“׳“׳•׳×.(Decoupled Subsystems) ׳”׳₪׳™׳×׳•׳— ׳‘׳•׳¦׳¢ ׳‘׳©׳׳‘׳™׳ ׳׳•׳’׳“׳¨׳™׳: ׳×׳—׳™׳׳” ׳ ׳‘׳ ׳×׳” ׳¡׳‘׳™׳‘׳× ׳”׳¡׳™׳׳•׳׳¦׳™׳” ׳•׳”׳׳׳’׳•׳¨׳™׳×׳, ׳׳׳—׳¨ ׳׳›׳ ׳©׳•׳׳‘ ׳׳•׳“׳ ׳”׳¨׳׳™׳™׳” ׳”׳׳׳•׳—׳©׳‘׳×, ׳•׳‘׳©׳׳‘ ׳”׳¡׳•׳₪׳™ ׳₪׳•׳×׳— ׳׳׳©׳§ ׳”׳ ׳™׳”׳•׳, ׳›׳׳©׳¨ ׳”׳×׳§׳©׳•׳¨׳× ׳‘׳™׳ ׳™׳”׳ ׳׳×׳‘׳¦׳¢׳× ׳“׳¨׳ ׳₪׳¨׳•׳˜׳•׳§׳•׳׳™׳ ׳׳•׳’׳“׳¨׳™׳ (׳›׳׳• WebSockets).

[182] (Normal) ׳”׳₪׳×׳¨׳•׳ ׳”׳™׳™׳©׳•׳׳™ :׳”׳׳×׳’׳¨ ׳ ׳₪׳×׳¨ ׳¢׳ ׳™׳“׳™ ׳×׳›׳ ׳•׳ ׳•׳‘׳ ׳™׳™׳” ׳©׳ ׳׳¢׳¨׳›׳× ׳¡׳™׳׳•׳׳¦׳™׳” ׳׳‘׳•׳§׳¨׳× ׳•׳¨׳—׳‘׳” ׳”׳׳™׳™׳¦׳¨׳× ׳×׳¨׳—׳™׳©׳™׳ ׳¨׳™׳׳׳™׳™׳ ׳׳•׳¨׳›׳‘׳™׳. ׳”׳¡׳™׳׳•׳׳˜׳•׳¨ ׳ ׳‘׳ ׳” ׳›׳ ׳©׳”׳•׳ ׳׳“׳׳” ׳ ׳׳׳ ׳” ׳׳× ׳×׳ ׳׳™ ׳”׳׳¦׳™׳׳•׳×, ׳׳¨׳‘׳•׳× ׳©׳¢׳•׳× ׳¢׳•׳׳¡ ׳׳•׳’׳“׳¨׳•׳× (׳₪׳™׳§ ׳‘׳‘׳•׳§׳¨/׳׳—׳¨ ׳”׳¦׳”׳¨׳™׳™׳), ׳©׳¢׳•׳× ׳©׳₪׳ (׳×׳ ׳•׳¢׳” ׳“׳׳™׳׳” ׳‘׳׳™׳׳”), ׳›׳ ׳™׳¡׳” ׳₪׳×׳׳•׳׳™׳× ׳•׳׳¡׳™׳ ׳›׳¨׳•׳ ׳™׳× ׳©׳ ׳¨׳›׳‘׳™ ׳—׳™׳¨׳•׳, ׳•׳‘׳§׳©׳•׳× ׳”׳“׳“׳™׳•׳× ׳׳¦׳׳×׳™׳ ׳©׳›׳ ׳™׳, ׳“׳‘׳¨ ׳©׳׳₪׳©׳¨ ׳׳‘׳—׳•׳ ׳׳× ׳—׳¡׳™׳ ׳•׳× ׳”׳׳¢׳¨׳›׳×.(Stress Testing)

[337] (a7) ׳”׳•׳’׳ ׳•׳× ׳‘׳™׳ ׳ ׳×׳™׳‘׳™׳ (Fairness): ׳׳ ׳™׳¢׳× ׳׳¦׳‘׳™ ׳§׳™׳¦׳•׳ ׳©׳‘׳”׳ ׳ ׳×׳™׳‘׳™׳ ׳׳©׳ ׳™׳™׳ ׳ ׳•׳×׳¨׳™׳ ׳‘׳׳•׳¨ ׳׳“׳•׳ ׳׳׳•׳¨׳ ׳–׳׳ ׳׳׳•׳©׳ (Starvation).

[621] (Normal) ׳׳™׳–׳•׳ ׳”׳×׳ ׳”׳’׳•׳×׳™ ׳‘׳׳׳¦׳¢׳•׳× ׳¢׳™׳¦׳•׳‘ ׳₪׳•׳ ׳§׳¦׳™׳™׳× ׳”׳×׳’׳׳•׳ (Reward Shaping):ֲ ׳”׳—׳׳× ׳§׳ ׳¡׳•׳× ׳׳×׳׳˜׳™׳™׳ ׳¢׳ ׳׳¦׳‘׳™ "׳¨׳¢׳‘" ׳§׳™׳¦׳•׳ ׳™׳™׳ ׳©׳ ׳ ׳×׳™׳‘׳™׳ (Starvation), ׳§׳ ׳¡׳•׳× ׳₪׳¨׳•׳₪׳•׳¨׳¦׳™׳•׳ ׳׳™׳™׳ ׳׳’׳™׳“׳•׳ ׳‘׳׳•׳¨׳ ׳”׳×׳•׳¨׳™׳, ׳•׳׳×׳ ׳‘׳•׳ ׳•׳¡׳™׳ ׳—׳™׳•׳‘׳™׳™׳ ׳¢׳ ׳”׳₪׳—׳×׳× ׳¢׳•׳׳¡׳™׳ ׳•׳׳§׳¡׳•׳ ׳”׳§׳™׳‘׳•׳׳× (Throughput).

[633] (2) 13.4 ׳ ׳•׳¡׳—׳׳•׳× ׳•׳¨׳©׳×׳•׳× ׳׳׳™׳“׳” (Mathematical Formulations)

[635] (NormalWeb) ׳¢׳“׳›׳•׳ ׳׳˜׳¨׳™׳¦׳× ׳”׳¢׳¨׳›׳™׳ ׳׳×׳‘׳¦׳¢ ׳‘׳›׳ ׳₪׳¡׳™׳¢׳× ׳–׳׳ (Time-Step) ׳‘׳”׳×׳׳ ׳׳׳©׳•׳•׳׳× ׳‘׳׳׳ (Bellman Equation) ׳”׳‘׳׳”:

[643] (NormalWeb) ׳׳“׳™׳ ׳™׳•׳× ׳”׳‘׳—׳™׳¨׳” ׳׳•׳’׳“׳¨׳× ׳׳×׳׳˜׳™׳× ׳¢׳ ׳₪׳™ ׳₪׳•׳ ׳§׳¦׳™׳™׳× ׳”׳¡׳×׳‘׳¨׳•׳× ׳׳•׳×׳ ׳™׳× ׳”׳׳•׳©׳₪׳¢׳× ׳׳¢׳¨׳ Epsilon ׳“׳™׳ ׳׳™:

[649] (Normal) ׳¨׳•׳‘׳“ ׳”׳¨׳׳™׳™׳” ׳”׳׳׳•׳—׳©׳‘׳× (Vision Layer):ֲ ׳‘׳©׳›׳‘׳× ׳”׳§׳׳˜ ׳”׳׳–׳”׳” ׳¨׳›׳‘׳™׳, ׳׳™׳•׳©׳ ׳׳•׳“׳ ׳׳׳™׳“׳” ׳¢׳׳•׳§׳” ׳׳¡׳•׳’ֲ YOLO (You Only Look Once). ׳׳•׳“׳ ׳–׳” ׳׳•׳₪׳¢׳ ׳‘׳׳¡׳’׳¨׳× ׳¨׳›׳™׳‘ ׳”-Vision ׳›׳“׳™ ׳׳‘׳¦׳¢ ׳’׳™׳׳•׳™, ׳¡׳™׳•׳•׳’ ׳•׳¡׳₪׳™׳¨׳” ׳©׳ ׳¢׳¦׳׳™׳ ׳‘׳–׳׳ ׳׳׳×, ׳•׳ ׳×׳•׳ ׳™ ׳”׳₪׳׳˜ ׳©׳׳• ׳׳•׳–׳¨׳§׳™׳ ׳™׳©׳™׳¨׳•׳× ׳›׳׳“׳“׳™ ׳ ׳₪׳— ׳׳•׳§׳׳™׳™׳ ׳׳ ׳•׳§׳˜׳•׳¨ ׳”׳׳¦׳‘ ׳©׳ ׳¡׳•׳›׳ ׳”-RL.

[1129] (Normal) ׳‘׳׳¡׳“ ׳”׳ ׳×׳•׳ ׳™׳ ׳׳•׳’׳“׳¨׳™׳ ׳׳™׳׳•׳¦׳™ ׳™׳™׳—׳•׳“׳™׳•׳× (UNIQUE) ׳•׳׳™׳׳•׳¦׳™ ׳‘׳“׳™׳§׳” (CHECK) ׳§׳©׳™׳—׳™׳ ׳”׳׳•׳ ׳¢׳™׳ ׳”׳–׳ ׳× ׳ ׳×׳•׳ ׳™׳ ׳׳ ׳×׳§׳™׳ ׳™׳. ׳‘׳˜׳‘׳׳× lane_conflicts׳ ׳©׳׳¨׳× ׳™׳™׳—׳•׳“׳™׳•׳× ׳׳©׳•׳׳‘׳× ׳©׳ ׳–׳•׳’׳•׳× ׳ ׳×׳™׳‘׳™׳ ׳‘׳×׳•׳ ׳׳•׳×׳• ׳¦׳•׳׳×, ׳׳¦׳“ ׳׳™׳׳•׳¥ ׳‘׳“׳™׳§׳” CHECK (lane_id_1 < lane_id_2) ׳”׳׳‘׳˜׳™׳— ׳¡׳“׳¨ ׳¢׳§׳‘׳™ ׳•׳׳•׳ ׳¢ ׳׳¦׳‘ ׳©׳‘׳• ׳ ׳×׳™׳‘ ׳™׳•׳’׳“׳¨ ׳‘׳§׳•׳ ׳₪׳׳™׳§׳˜ ׳¢׳ ׳¢׳¦׳׳•. ׳‘׳׳•׳₪׳ ׳“׳•׳׳”, ׳‘׳˜׳‘׳׳× intersection_neighbors׳׳•׳’׳“׳¨ ׳׳™׳׳•׳¥ ׳™׳™׳—׳•׳“׳™׳•׳× ׳׳©׳•׳׳‘ ׳”׳׳•׳ ׳¢ ׳¨׳™׳©׳•׳ ׳›׳₪׳•׳ ׳©׳ ׳׳•׳×׳• ׳§׳©׳¨ ׳©׳›׳ ׳•׳× ׳‘׳™׳ ׳©׳ ׳™ ׳¦׳׳×׳™׳. ׳”׳©׳“׳•׳× ׳”׳’׳™׳׳•׳’׳¨׳₪׳™׳™׳, ׳©׳“׳•׳× ׳”׳›׳™׳•׳•׳ ׳™׳ ׳‘׳˜׳‘׳׳× ׳”׳ ׳×׳™׳‘׳™׳ (׳”׳׳•׳’׳‘׳׳™׳ ׳‘׳׳׳¦׳¢׳•׳× ׳׳™׳׳•׳¥ ׳§׳©׳™׳— ׳¨׳§ ׳׳›׳™׳•׳•׳ ׳™ ׳”׳©׳׳™׳™׳ ׳”׳×׳§׳ ׳™׳™׳) ׳•׳©׳“׳•׳× ׳”׳×׳¦׳•׳¨׳” ׳׳•׳’׳“׳¨׳™׳ ׳׳₪׳™ ׳˜׳™׳₪׳•׳¡׳™ ׳ ׳×׳•׳ ׳™׳ ׳׳“׳•׳™׳§׳™׳, ׳”׳׳‘׳˜׳™׳—׳™׳ ׳›׳™ ׳”׳׳¢׳¨׳›׳× ׳×׳¢׳‘׳“ ׳׳ ׳•׳¨׳§ ׳׳™׳“׳¢ ׳×׳§׳™׳ ׳•׳׳ ׳•׳¨׳׳.

[1360] (Normal) ׳¢׳•׳׳¡ ׳©׳׳™׳׳×׳•׳× ׳׳•׳ ׳׳¡׳“ ׳”׳ ׳×׳•׳ ׳™׳: ׳©׳›׳‘׳× ׳”-ODBC ׳‘-C++ ׳”׳¦׳™׳’׳” ׳™׳¦׳™׳‘׳•׳× ׳׳׳׳” ׳•׳¢׳׳“׳” ׳‘׳§׳¦׳‘ ׳©׳ ׳¢׳“ 200 ׳©׳׳™׳׳×׳•׳× ׳§׳¨׳™׳׳”/׳›׳×׳™׳‘׳” ׳‘׳©׳ ׳™׳™׳” ׳׳•׳ ׳”-SQL Server, ׳׳׳ ׳”׳’׳¢׳” ׳׳׳¦׳‘׳™ ׳ ׳¢׳™׳׳× ׳¨׳©׳•׳׳•׳× ׳•׳׳׳ ׳—׳¨׳™׳’׳” ׳׳׳©׳׳‘׳™ ׳”׳–׳™׳›׳¨׳•׳ ׳•׳”׳׳¢׳‘׳“ ׳©׳ ׳©׳¨׳× ׳”׳׳™׳‘׳”.

[1362] (Normal) ׳₪׳¨׳§ ׳–׳” ׳׳¨׳›׳– ׳׳× ׳”׳‘׳“׳™׳§׳•׳× ׳”׳₪׳•׳ ׳§׳¦׳™׳•׳ ׳׳™׳•׳× ׳”׳§׳©׳™׳—׳•׳× ׳©׳‘׳•׳¦׳¢׳• ׳¢׳ ׳׳ ׳× ׳׳×׳§׳£ ׳׳× ׳׳ ׳’׳ ׳•׳ ׳™ ׳׳‘׳˜׳—׳× ׳”׳׳™׳“׳¢, ׳©׳׳׳•׳× ׳”׳ ׳×׳•׳ ׳™׳ ׳•׳”׳₪׳¨׳“׳× ׳”׳×׳₪׳§׳™׳“׳™׳ ׳‘׳׳¢׳¨׳›׳×:
[1363] (Normal) ׳‘׳“׳™׳§׳× ׳—׳¡׳™׳׳× ׳׳™׳׳•׳× ׳©׳’׳•׳™ (Brute Force / Invalid Auth): ׳‘׳•׳¦׳¢׳• ׳ ׳™׳¡׳™׳•׳ ׳•׳× ׳”׳×׳—׳‘׳¨׳•׳× ׳™׳–׳•׳׳™׳ ׳¢׳ ׳©׳׳•׳× ׳׳©׳×׳׳© ׳©׳׳™׳ ׳ ׳§׳™׳™׳׳™׳ ׳•׳¡׳™׳¡׳׳׳•׳× ׳©׳’׳•׳™׳•׳×. ׳”׳©׳¨׳× ׳“׳—׳” ׳׳× ׳›׳׳ ׳”׳‘׳§׳©׳•׳× ׳¢׳ ׳§׳•׳“ ׳©׳’׳™׳׳”, ׳׳ ׳¢ ׳”׳ ׳₪׳§׳× ׳˜׳•׳§׳, ׳•׳׳׳©׳§ ׳”׳׳§׳•׳— ׳—׳¡׳ ׳׳× ׳”׳ ׳™׳×׳•׳‘ ׳•׳”׳¦׳™׳’ ׳”׳×׳¨׳¢׳” ׳׳“׳•׳׳”.
[1364] (Normal) ׳‘׳“׳™׳§׳× ׳”׳’׳ ׳× ׳ ׳§׳•׳“׳•׳× ׳§׳¦׳” (Endpoint Protection): ׳‘׳•׳¦׳¢ ׳ ׳™׳¡׳™׳•׳ ׳׳©׳׳™׳—׳× ׳‘׳§׳©׳× HTTP POST ׳׳¢׳“׳›׳•׳ ׳×׳¦׳•׳¨׳× ׳ ׳×׳™׳‘׳™׳ ׳™׳©׳™׳¨׳•׳× ׳-API ׳©׳ ׳©׳¨׳× ׳”-C++ (׳‘׳׳׳¦׳¢׳•׳× ׳›׳׳™ ׳¦׳“-׳©׳׳™׳©׳™ ׳¢׳•׳§׳£ ׳׳׳©׳§ UI), ׳׳׳ ׳”׳›׳׳׳× ׳׳¡׳™׳׳•׳ ׳”-JWT ׳‘׳›׳•׳×׳¨׳•׳× ׳”׳‘׳§׳©׳” (Headers). ׳”׳©׳¨׳× ׳–׳™׳”׳” ׳׳× ׳”׳™׳¢׳“׳¨ ׳”׳˜׳•׳§׳, ׳—׳¡׳ ׳׳× ׳”׳₪׳ ׳™׳™׳” ׳‘׳׳•׳₪׳ ׳׳˜׳•׳׳™ ׳•׳”׳—׳–׳™׳¨ ׳§׳•׳“ ׳×׳’׳•׳‘׳” ׳§׳©׳™׳— 401 Unauthorized. ׳”׳©׳™׳ ׳•׳™ ׳׳ ׳ ׳¨׳©׳ ׳‘׳׳¡׳“ ׳”׳ ׳×׳•׳ ׳™׳.
[1365] (Normal) ׳‘׳“׳™׳§׳× ׳”׳₪׳¨׳“׳× ׳”׳¨׳©׳׳•׳× ׳×׳₪׳§׳™׳“׳™׳ (Role Isolation): ׳‘׳•׳¦׳¢׳” ׳”׳×׳—׳‘׳¨׳•׳× ׳¢׳ ׳—׳©׳‘׳•׳ ׳”׳׳•׳’׳“׳¨ ׳›׳₪׳¨׳•׳₪׳™׳ ׳׳₪׳¢׳™׳ (Operator). ׳”׳׳¢׳¨׳›׳× ׳”׳¡׳×׳™׳¨׳” ׳‘׳¦׳•׳¨׳” ׳׳׳׳” ׳׳× ׳¨׳›׳™׳‘׳™ ׳׳¡׳ ׳”-AdminPage ׳‘׳׳׳©׳§ ׳”-React. ׳‘׳ ׳™׳¡׳™׳•׳ ׳™׳–׳•׳ ׳׳₪׳ ׳•׳× ׳™׳©׳™׳¨׳•׳× ׳׳›׳×׳•׳‘׳× ׳”-URL ׳©׳ ׳“׳£ ׳”׳ ׳™׳”׳•׳, ׳׳¢׳¨׳›׳× ׳”׳ ׳™׳×׳•׳‘ ׳‘׳¦׳“ ׳”׳׳§׳•׳— ׳–׳™׳”׳×׳” ׳׳× ׳”׳×׳₪׳§׳™׳“ ׳•׳‘׳™׳¦׳¢׳” ׳—׳¡׳™׳׳” ׳•׳ ׳™׳×׳•׳‘ ׳׳—׳“׳© ׳׳“׳£ ׳”׳‘׳™׳×.
[1366] (Normal) ׳‘׳“׳™׳§׳× ׳׳™׳׳•׳¦׳™ ׳©׳׳׳•׳× ׳ ׳×׳•׳ ׳™׳ ׳‘-Database: ׳‘׳•׳¦׳¢ ׳ ׳™׳¡׳™׳•׳ ׳׳›׳•׳•׳ ׳׳”׳–׳ ׳× ׳§׳©׳¨ ׳©׳›׳ ׳•׳× ׳‘׳™׳ ׳¦׳•׳׳× ׳׳¢׳¦׳׳• ׳•׳ ׳™׳¡׳™׳•׳ ׳׳”׳–׳ ׳× ׳׳¨׳—׳§ ׳©׳׳™׳׳™ (-150 ׳׳˜׳¨׳™׳). ׳׳¡׳“ ׳”׳ ׳×׳•׳ ׳™׳ ׳”׳₪׳¢׳™׳ ׳׳× ׳׳™׳׳•׳¦׳™ ׳”-CHECK ׳”׳׳•׳‘׳ ׳™׳ ׳©׳׳•, ׳”׳“׳£ ׳׳× ׳”׳©׳׳™׳׳×׳”, ׳׳ ׳¢ ׳׳× ׳¨׳™׳©׳•׳ ׳”׳¨׳©׳•׳׳” ׳”׳×׳§׳•׳׳” ׳•׳”׳—׳–׳™׳¨ ׳©׳’׳™׳׳× ׳”׳₪׳¨׳× ׳׳™׳׳•׳¥ ׳©׳׳׳•׳× ׳©׳”׳•׳¦׳’׳” ׳׳׳ ׳”׳ ׳›׳”׳•׳“׳¢׳” ׳׳“׳•׳׳”.

[1368] (Normal) ׳‘׳”׳×׳‘׳¡׳¡ ׳¢׳ ׳׳¢׳¨׳ ׳”׳‘׳“׳™׳§׳•׳× ׳”׳¨׳‘-׳©׳›׳‘׳×׳™ ׳”׳׳§׳™׳£ ׳©׳‘׳•׳¦׳¢, ׳”׳׳¢׳¨׳›׳× ׳¢׳•׳׳“׳× ׳‘׳׳•׳₪׳ ׳׳׳ ׳‘׳›׳ ׳”׳“׳¨׳™׳©׳•׳× ׳”׳₪׳•׳ ׳§׳¦׳™׳•׳ ׳׳™׳•׳×, ׳”׳׳‘׳¦׳¢׳™׳•׳× ׳•׳”׳˜׳›׳ ׳•׳׳•׳’׳™׳•׳× ׳©׳”׳•׳’׳“׳¨׳• ׳¢׳‘׳•׳¨ ׳₪׳¨׳•׳™׳§׳˜ ׳”׳’׳׳¨:

[1379] (Normal) ׳׳—׳× ׳”׳׳¡׳§׳ ׳•׳× ׳”׳׳¨׳›׳–׳™׳•׳× ׳•׳”׳—׳©׳•׳‘׳•׳× ׳‘׳™׳•׳×׳¨ ׳׳×׳”׳׳™׳ ׳”׳₪׳™׳×׳•׳— ׳”׳™׳ ׳©׳§׳‘׳™׳¢׳× ׳׳¨׳›׳™׳˜׳§׳˜׳•׳¨׳” ׳§׳©׳™׳—׳” ׳•׳׳•׳“׳•׳׳¨׳™׳× ׳׳¨׳׳© ׳׳§׳˜׳™׳ ׳” ׳׳©׳׳¢׳•׳×׳™׳× ׳׳× ׳¡׳™׳›׳•׳ ׳™ ׳”׳׳™׳ ׳˜׳’׳¨׳¦׳™׳” ׳‘׳©׳׳‘׳™׳ ׳”׳׳×׳§׳“׳׳™׳. ׳™׳™׳©׳•׳ ׳§׳₪׳“׳ ׳™ ׳©׳ ׳¢׳§׳¨׳•׳ ׳”׳₪׳¨׳“׳× ׳”׳׳—׳¨׳™׳•׳× (Separation of Concerns) ׳‘׳™׳ ׳©׳›׳‘׳× ׳”׳×׳¦׳•׳’׳” (React), ׳©׳›׳‘׳× ׳”׳׳•׳’׳™׳§׳” ׳•׳”׳׳™׳ ׳˜׳’׳¨׳¦׳™׳” (C++ Server & FastAPI) ׳•׳©׳›׳‘׳× ׳”׳ ׳×׳•׳ ׳™׳ (SQL Server) ׳׳₪׳©׳¨ ׳×׳—׳–׳•׳§׳× ׳§׳•׳“ ׳™׳¢׳™׳׳”, ׳‘׳™׳“׳•׳“ ׳•׳׳™׳×׳•׳¨ ׳×׳§׳׳•׳× ׳׳”׳™׳¨ (Fault Isolation), ׳•׳™׳›׳•׳׳× ׳”׳¨׳—׳‘׳” ׳׳“׳•׳¨׳’׳× (Scalability) ׳©׳ ׳¨׳›׳™׳‘׳™ ׳”׳׳¢׳¨׳›׳× ׳׳‘׳׳™ ׳׳”׳©׳₪׳™׳¢ ׳¢׳ ׳™׳¦׳™׳‘׳•׳× ׳”׳׳™׳‘׳”.

[1381] (Normal) ׳ ׳™׳”׳•׳ ׳¢׳“׳›׳•׳ ׳™׳ ׳¨׳¦׳™׳₪׳™׳ ׳•׳׳¡׳™׳ ׳›׳¨׳•׳ ׳™׳™׳ ׳׳¢׳׳“׳•׳× ׳”׳׳₪׳¢׳™׳ ׳׳—׳™׳™׳‘ ׳×׳›׳ ׳•׳ ׳׳“׳•׳™׳§ ׳©׳ ׳–׳¨׳™׳׳× ׳”׳ ׳×׳•׳ ׳™׳ ׳•׳׳ ׳™׳¢׳× ׳¦׳•׳•׳׳¨׳™ ׳‘׳§׳‘׳•׳§. ׳”׳©׳™׳׳•׳‘ ׳”׳׳¨׳›׳™׳˜׳§׳˜׳•׳ ׳™ ׳”׳׳‘׳•׳–׳¨ ׳‘׳™׳ ׳‘׳§׳©׳•׳× API ׳¡׳˜׳ ׳“׳¨׳˜׳™׳•׳× (HTTP) ׳׳‘׳™׳ ׳“׳—׳™׳₪׳× ׳ ׳×׳•׳ ׳™׳ ׳¨׳¦׳™׳₪׳” ׳‘׳¢׳¨׳•׳¦׳™ ׳–׳׳ ׳׳׳× (WebSockets) ׳”׳•׳›׳™׳— ׳׳× ׳¢׳¦׳׳• ׳›׳₪׳×׳¨׳•׳ ׳׳•׳₪׳˜׳™׳׳׳™. ׳’׳™׳©׳” ׳–׳• ׳׳₪׳©׳¨׳” ׳׳”׳¦׳™׳’ ׳×׳׳•׳ ׳× ׳׳¦׳‘ ׳׳—׳™׳“׳”, ׳׳¡׳•׳ ׳›׳¨׳ ׳× ׳•׳¢׳“׳›׳ ׳™׳× ׳©׳ ׳”׳¦׳׳×׳™׳ ׳‘׳—׳“׳¨ ׳”׳‘׳§׳¨׳”, ׳×׳•׳ ׳©׳׳™׳¨׳” ׳¢׳ ׳¨׳¦׳™׳₪׳•׳× ׳×׳₪׳¢׳•׳׳™׳× ׳׳׳׳” ׳•׳—׳•׳•׳™׳™׳× ׳׳©׳×׳׳© ׳—׳׳§׳” ׳‘׳׳¡׳›׳™ ׳”׳ ׳™׳˜׳•׳¨ ׳•׳”׳©׳׳™׳˜׳” ׳‘׳׳¦׳‘׳™ ׳—׳™׳¨׳•׳.

[1383] (Normal) ׳‘׳׳¢׳¨׳›׳•׳× ׳×׳•׳›׳ ׳” ׳׳‘׳•׳–׳¨׳•׳× ׳•׳׳•׳¨׳›׳‘׳•׳×, ׳‘׳“׳™׳§׳•׳× ׳™׳—׳™׳“׳” (Unit Tests) ׳׳‘׳•׳“׳“׳•׳× ׳׳™׳ ׳ ׳׳¡׳₪׳§׳•׳× ׳›׳׳™ ׳׳¡׳₪׳§ ׳׳”׳‘׳˜׳—׳× ׳—׳•׳¡׳ ׳”׳׳¢׳¨׳›׳×. ׳”׳¢׳¨׳ ׳”׳”׳ ׳“׳¡׳™ ׳”׳¢׳™׳§׳¨׳™ ׳•׳”׳׳›׳™׳“׳•׳× ׳”׳׳¢׳¨׳›׳×׳™׳× ׳”׳•׳©׳’׳• ׳‘׳׳׳¦׳¢׳•׳× ׳©׳™׳׳•׳‘ ׳׳•׳‘׳ ׳” ׳©׳ ׳‘׳“׳™׳§׳•׳× ׳׳™׳ ׳˜׳’׳¨׳¦׳™׳” ׳¨׳‘-׳©׳›׳‘׳×׳™׳•׳× ׳•׳‘׳“׳™׳§׳•׳× ׳§׳¦׳” ׳׳§׳¦׳” (E2E) ׳׳‘׳•׳¡׳¡׳•׳× ׳×׳¡׳¨׳™׳˜׳™׳ (Scenario-Based Testing). ׳’׳™׳©׳” ׳–׳• ׳׳₪׳©׳¨׳” ׳׳׳׳× ׳׳ ׳¨׳§ ׳₪׳•׳ ׳§׳¦׳™׳•׳× ׳׳•׳’׳™׳•׳× ׳ ׳§׳•׳“׳×׳™׳•׳×, ׳׳׳ ׳׳×׳§׳£ ׳׳× ׳©׳׳׳•׳× ׳¦׳™׳ ׳•׳¨ ׳”׳¢׳‘׳¨׳× ׳”׳ ׳×׳•׳ ׳™׳ ׳”׳׳׳ ׳•׳׳× ׳¢׳׳™׳“׳× ׳”׳׳¢׳¨׳›׳× ׳‘׳׳™׳׳•׳¦׳™ ׳–׳׳ ׳•׳×׳’׳•׳‘׳” ׳×׳—׳× ׳¢׳•׳׳¡.

[1385] (Normal) ׳׳‘׳˜׳—׳× ׳׳™׳“׳¢ ׳•׳”׳’׳ ׳× ׳ ׳×׳•׳ ׳™׳ ׳׳™׳ ׳ ׳׳”׳•׳•׳× ׳¨׳›׳™׳‘ ׳׳©׳׳™׳ ׳׳• ׳×׳•׳¡׳₪׳× ׳׳׳•׳—׳¨׳×, ׳׳׳ ׳¨׳›׳™׳‘ ׳×׳©׳×׳™׳×׳™ ׳׳•׳‘׳ ׳” ׳‘׳׳™׳‘׳× ׳”׳׳¨׳›׳™׳˜׳§׳˜׳•׳¨׳” (Security by Design). ׳׳ ׳’׳ ׳•׳ ׳™ ׳׳™׳׳•׳× ׳”׳׳©׳×׳׳©׳™׳, ׳”׳ ׳₪׳§׳× ׳˜׳•׳§׳ ׳™׳ ׳׳ ׳•׳”׳׳™׳ (JWT), ׳‘׳§׳¨׳” ׳§׳©׳™׳—׳” ׳¢׳ ׳ ׳§׳•׳“׳•׳× ׳§׳¦׳” (Endpoints) ׳”׳׳‘׳¦׳¢׳•׳× ׳₪׳¢׳•׳׳•׳× ׳¨׳’׳™׳©׳•׳×, ׳•׳”׳₪׳¨׳“׳” ׳׳•׳—׳׳˜׳× ׳©׳ ׳”׳¨׳©׳׳•׳× ׳‘׳™׳ ׳“׳¨׳’ ׳ ׳™׳”׳•׳׳™ (Admin) ׳׳“׳¨׳’ ׳×׳₪׳¢׳•׳׳™ (Operator), ׳×׳•׳›׳ ׳ ׳• ׳•׳׳•׳׳©׳• ׳›׳—׳׳§ ׳‘׳׳×׳™ ׳ ׳₪׳¨׳“ ׳׳’׳¨׳¢׳™׳ ׳”׳׳¢׳¨׳›׳×, ׳•׳‘׳›׳ ׳׳ ׳¢׳• ׳–׳׳™׳’׳× ׳׳™׳“׳¢ ׳׳• ׳¢׳§׳™׳₪׳× ׳¡׳׳›׳•׳™׳•׳×.

[1387] (Normal) ׳”׳׳¢׳¨׳›׳× ׳×׳•׳›׳ ׳ ׳” ׳•׳׳•׳׳©׳” ׳‘׳’׳™׳©׳” ׳”׳ ׳“׳¡׳™׳× ׳¨׳‘-׳¨׳›׳™׳‘׳™׳× ׳׳©׳•׳׳‘׳×, ׳”׳׳ ׳¦׳׳× ׳׳× ׳ ׳§׳•׳“׳•׳× ׳”׳—׳•׳–׳§ ׳©׳ ׳›׳ ׳˜׳›׳ ׳•׳׳•׳’׳™׳”:

[1390] (Normal) ׳©׳›׳‘׳•׳× ׳×׳׳™׳›׳”, ׳¡׳™׳׳•׳׳¦׳™׳” ׳•׳׳ ׳׳™׳˜׳™׳§׳” (Python Ecosystem):׳©׳™׳׳•׳© ׳‘׳¨׳›׳™׳‘׳™ Python ׳•׳‘׳׳¢׳¨׳›׳×FastAPI׳™׳—׳“ ׳¢׳ ׳¡׳₪׳¨׳™׳™׳× pyodbc׳׳¦׳•׳¨׳ ׳‘׳™׳¦׳•׳¢ ׳¡׳™׳׳•׳׳¦׳™׳•׳× ׳×׳ ׳•׳¢׳”, ׳”׳₪׳§׳× ׳׳“׳“׳™ KPI ׳×׳¢׳‘׳•׳¨׳×׳™׳™׳, ׳¢׳™׳‘׳•׳“ ׳—׳–׳•׳×׳™ ׳׳×׳§׳“׳, ׳•׳‘׳™׳¦׳•׳¢ ׳׳•׳’׳™׳§׳× ׳¨׳™׳©׳•׳ ׳•׳׳¢׳§׳‘ ׳§׳©׳™׳—׳” ׳‘׳‘׳¡׳™׳¡ ׳”׳ ׳×׳•׳ ׳™׳ ׳”׳™׳™׳¢׳•׳“׳™ FilesLog.
[1391] (Normal) ׳©׳›׳‘׳× ׳”׳ ׳×׳•׳ ׳™׳ (Database Layer):׳‘׳¡׳™׳¡ ׳ ׳×׳•׳ ׳™׳ ׳¨׳׳¦׳™׳•׳ ׳™ ׳׳ ׳•׳”׳ ׳‘-SQL Server. ׳”׳׳•׳“׳ ׳”׳ ׳×׳•׳ ׳™ ׳ ׳©׳¢׳ ׳¢׳ ׳׳¨׳›׳™׳˜׳§׳˜׳•׳¨׳× ׳˜׳‘׳׳׳•׳× ׳׳™׳‘׳” ׳׳ ׳•׳¨׳׳׳•׳× ׳”׳׳™׳™׳¦׳’׳•׳× ׳׳× ׳™׳©׳•׳™׳•׳× ׳”׳׳¢׳¨׳›׳×: ׳¦׳׳×׳™׳ (intersections), ׳ ׳×׳™׳‘׳™׳ (lanes), ׳׳˜׳¨׳™׳¦׳•׳× ׳§׳•׳ ׳₪׳׳™׳§׳˜׳™׳ (phase_conflicts), ׳©׳›׳ ׳•׳™׳•׳× ׳˜׳•׳₪׳•׳׳•׳’׳™׳•׳× (intersection_neighbors) ׳•׳׳©׳×׳׳©׳™ ׳׳¢׳¨׳›׳× (admin_users). ׳©׳׳׳•׳× ׳”׳׳™׳“׳¢ ׳•׳׳˜׳•׳׳™׳•׳× ׳”׳₪׳¢׳•׳׳•׳× ׳”׳•׳‘׳˜׳—׳• ׳‘׳׳׳¦׳¢׳•׳× ׳”׳’׳“׳¨׳× ׳׳₪׳×׳—׳•׳× ׳¨׳׳©׳™׳™׳, ׳׳₪׳×׳—׳•׳× ׳–׳¨׳™׳ (Foreign Keys) ׳•׳׳™׳׳•׳¦׳™ ׳×׳§׳™׳ ׳•׳× ׳§׳©׳™׳—׳™׳ (CHECK Constraints).
